3D Printing of Iron-Based Lattice Structures Produced by Selective Laser Melting

Medium AM Biomedical Materials openalex Transferable Skim only none
А. V. Minitskyi, O. V. Stepanov, S. V. Radchuk, Ievgen Byba, П. І. Лобода Powder Metallurgy and Metal Ceramics 2022-07-01 10.1007/s11106-022-00306-9
单位: National Technical University ‘Igor Sikorsky Kyiv Polytechnic Institute, Kyiv, Ukraine
通讯: А. V. Minitskyi @ National Technical University ‘Igor Sikorsky Kyiv Polytechnic Institute, Kyiv, Ukraine
相关性: 该论文研究铁基晶格结构的SLM制造，可能用于可降解生物植入物，但未明确涉及应力遮挡或疲劳性能。
边界: It does not explicitly address stress shielding, fatigue testing, or osseointegration, and lacks biomedical validation.
动机· 探索铁基晶格结构在生物植入物中的应用潜力
方法· 采用选择性激光熔化技术制备铁基晶格样品
结果· 成功制备了具有可控孔隙结构的铁基晶格
验证· 未提及具体力学或生物验证
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To investigate the feasibility of iron-based lattice structures for potential biomedical implant applications.
Method· Selective laser melting was employed to fabricate iron-based lattice structures with designed porosity.
Result· The fabricated lattices exhibited controllable porous architecture suitable for further evaluation.
Validation· No experimental validation of mechanical or biological performance was reported.
selective laser melting · 选择性激光熔化lattice structure · 晶格结构iron-based · 铁基biodegradable implant · 可降解植入物porosity · 孔隙率
SLM iron-based lattice biodegradable implant porous structure
标记：

Predicting defects in laser powder bed fusion using in-situ thermal imaging data and machine learning

Medium AM Biomedical Materials openalex Transferable Save for project simulation_case Exp. validation
Sina Malakpour Estalaki, Cody Lough, Robert G. Landers, Edward C. Kinzel, Tengfei Luo Additive manufacturing 2022-07-01 10.1016/j.addma.2022.103008
单位: Department of Aerospace and Mechanical Engineering, University of Notre Dame, Notre Dame, IN 46556, USA
通讯: Tengfei Luo @ Department of Aerospace and Mechanical Engineering, University of Notre Dame, Notre Dame, IN 46556, USA
相关性: 该论文使用机器学习预测LPBF过程中的缺陷，方法可迁移至AM生物医学植入物的质量监控，但未涉及多孔结构、应力遮挡或疲劳测试。
边界: This paper does not address porous structure design, stress shielding, mechanical testing (static or fatigue), or osseointegration for biomedical implants.
动机· 利用热成像数据和机器学习预测LPBF过程中的缺陷。
方法· 结合热成像数据与机器学习模型预测缺陷位置和类型。
结果· 模型能有效预测常见缺陷，如孔隙和裂纹。
验证· 通过实验打印件与预测结果对比验证。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To predict defects in laser powder bed fusion using in-situ thermal imaging data and machine learning for improved process control.
Method· Machine learning models were trained on thermal imaging data to classify and localize defects during LPBF.
Result· The models achieved high accuracy in predicting porosity and crack defects.
Validation· Validation was performed by comparing model predictions with actual defects observed in printed parts.
laser powder bed fusion · 激光粉末床熔融defect prediction · 缺陷预测machine learning · 机器学习thermal imaging · 热成像in-situ monitoring · 原位监测
LPBF defect prediction machine learning thermal imaging in-situ monitoring
标记：

A convolutional neural network-based multi-sensor fusion approach for in-situ quality monitoring of selective laser melting

Medium AM Biomedical Materials openalex Transferable Skim only simulation_case Exp. validation
Jingchang Li, Qi Zhou, Longchao Cao, Yanzhi Wang, Jiexiang Hu Journal of Manufacturing Systems 2022-07-01 10.1016/j.jmsy.2022.07.007
单位: School of Aerospace Engineering, Huazhong University of Science & Technology, 430074, Wuhan, PR China
通讯: Jiexiang Hu @ School of Aerospace Engineering, Huazhong University of Science & Technology, 430074, Wuhan, PR China
相关性: 该论文关注SLM过程监控而非生物医学植入物，但多传感器融合方法可迁移至AM生物医学植入物的质量监测。
边界: No porous structure design, no stress shielding analysis, no mechanical testing (static or fatigue), no surface roughness or osseointegration data.
动机· 实现SLM过程实时质量监控，减少缺陷。
方法· 提出CNN多传感器融合方法，处理熔池图像和声发射信号。
结果· 融合方法提高了缺陷检测准确率。
验证· 在SLM实验平台上验证，与单传感器对比。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To achieve real-time quality monitoring in selective laser melting for defect reduction.
Method· A convolutional neural network-based multi-sensor fusion approach integrating melt pool images and acoustic emission signals.
Result· The fusion method improves defect detection accuracy compared to single-sensor approaches.
Validation· Validated on an experimental SLM setup with comparison to single-sensor baselines.
selective laser melting · 选择性激光熔化in-situ monitoring · 原位监测convolutional neural network · 卷积神经网络multi-sensor fusion · 多传感器融合quality monitoring · 质量监测
selective laser melting in-situ monitoring convolutional neural network multi-sensor fusion quality monitoring
标记：

Exploring the duality of powder adhesion and underlying surface roughness in laser powder bed fusion processed Ti-6Al-4V

Medium AM Biomedical Materials openalex Peripheral Skim only mechanical_testing Exp. validation
Luke N. Carter, Victor M. Villapún, Liam M. Grover, Sophie C. Cox Journal of Manufacturing Processes 2022-07-01 10.1016/j.jmapro.2022.06.057
单位: Healthcare Technologies Institute, School of Chemical Engineering, University of Birmingham, Birmingham B15 2TT, UK
通讯: Luke N. Carter @ Healthcare Technologies Institute, School of Chemical Engineering, University of Birmingham, Birmingham B15 2TT, UK
相关性: 该论文研究了LPBF Ti-6Al-4V的表面粗糙度和粉末粘附，与生物医学植入物的表面形貌相关，但未涉及应力遮挡或疲劳性能。
边界: This paper does not link porous structure to stress shielding or stiffness matching, lacks mechanical testing (fatigue or static), and does not address osseointegration.
动机· 理解LPBF Ti-6Al-4V表面粗糙度的双重结构（粘附粉末与基体轮廓）
方法· 通过光学图像分析和SEM图像处理量化粉末粘附和基体粗糙度
结果· 表面粗糙度和粉末粘附随能量输入降低，粉末粒径影响粘附但基体粗糙度影响小
验证· 与传统线粗糙度测量对比，并通过不同粉末粒径实验验证
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To understand the dual structure of as-built surface roughness in LPBF Ti-6Al-4V, consisting of adhered powder and underlying consolidated material.
Method· Optical image analysis and SEM-based image processing were used to quantify powder adhesion and underlying surface profile under varying contour scan parameters.
Result· Surface finish and powder adhesion decreased with energy input; finer powder reduced overall roughness but increased particle adhesion density.
Validation· Results were compared with traditional line-of-sight roughness measurements and validated using coarse vs. fine powder feedstock experiments.
laser powder bed fusion · 激光粉末床熔融surface roughness · 表面粗糙度powder adhesion · 粉末粘附Ti-6Al-4V · Ti-6Al-4Vcontour scan parameters · 轮廓扫描参数optical image analysis · 光学图像分析underlying surface profile · 基体表面轮廓argon crossflow · 氩气横流
LPBF surface roughness powder adhesion Ti-6Al-4V process parameters
标记：

Deep neural network based reduced-order model for fluid–structure interaction system

Medium FEA & Surrogate Modelling openalex Transferable Save for project numerical_benchmark
Renkun Han, Yixing Wang, Weiqi Qian, Wenzheng Wang, Miao Zhang et al. Physics of Fluids 2022-07-01 10.1063/5.0096432
单位: China Aerodynamics Research and Development Center, Mianyang 621000, China
通讯: Gang Chen @ State Key Laboratory for Strength and Vibration of Mechanical Structures, Shannxi Key Laboratory for Environment and Con
相关性: 该论文提出的基于深度神经网络的降阶模型可加速流固耦合仿真，其方法可迁移至生物力学中植入物-骨界面的快速仿真与优化。
边界: This paper focuses on fluid-structure interaction systems, not on solid mechanics or implant-bone interface modeling, and lacks uncertainty quantification and constraint handling.
动机· 流固耦合仿真计算成本高，优化时需大量采样，亟需快速预测方法。
方法· 构建深度神经网络降阶模型，基于当前流场和结构运动预测下一时刻流场，并与结构动力学求解器耦合。
结果· 模型在参数范围内预测精度与数值模拟吻合，仿真速度提升20倍以上。
验证· 与数值模拟结果对比验证预测精度。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· High computational cost of fluid-structure interaction simulations hinders optimization over large parameter spaces.
Method· A deep neural network reduced-order model predicts the next-step flow field from current flow and structural motion, coupled with a structural dynamics solver.
Result· The model achieves good agreement with numerical simulations within the trained parameter range and accelerates simulation by over 20 times.
Validation· Validation against numerical simulation results.
reduced-order model · 降阶模型deep neural network · 深度神经网络fluid-structure interaction · 流固耦合computational fluid dynamics · 计算流体动力学surrogate model · 代理模型parameter space · 参数空间prediction accuracy · 预测精度simulation speedup · 仿真加速
reduced-order model deep neural network fluid-structure interaction surrogate modeling computational efficiency
标记：

Twin-Model Based on Model Order Reduction for Rotating Motors

Medium FEA & Surrogate Modelling openalex Transferable Save for project simulation_case
Ze Guo, Shuai Yan, Xiaoyu Xu, Zhifu Chen, Zhuoxiang Ren IEEE Transactions on Magnetics 2022-07-01 10.1109/tmag.2022.3187620
单位: Institute of Electrical Engineering, Chinese Academy of Sciences, Beijing, China
通讯: Ze Guo @ Institute of Electrical Engineering, Chinese Academy of Sciences, Beijing, China
相关性: 该论文将模型降阶技术应用于电机实时仿真，其方法可迁移至生物力学植入物设计的代理建模。
边界: This paper does not address biomedical implant design, contact mechanics, or uncertainty quantification; it focuses on rotating motors in electromagnetics.
动机· 电机参数空间仿真数据生成耗时，需快速行为模型。
方法· 基于POD的模型降阶构建紧凑行为模型，并与机械方程耦合。
结果· 降阶模型在保证精度的同时大幅缩短仿真时间。
验证· 与有限元仿真对比时域响应（速度、转矩、功角）。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· Generating simulation data in a parametric space for electric motors is time-consuming, necessitating efficient behavior models.
Method· A model order reduction technique based on proper orthogonal decomposition is used to build a compact behavior model, which is then coupled with mechanical rotation equations to form a twin model.
Result· The twin model provides accurate time-domain responses (speed, torque, power angle) compared to FEM, with negligible simulation time.
Validation· Validation is performed by comparing the reduced-order model's time-domain outputs against full finite-element simulations.
model order reduction · 模型降阶proper orthogonal decomposition · 本征正交分解digital twin · 数字孪生real-time simulation · 实时仿真finite element method · 有限元法
model order reduction proper orthogonal decomposition digital twin real-time simulation rotating motors
标记：

Multifidelity uncertainty quantification and model validation of large-scale multidisciplinary systems

Medium FEA & Surrogate Modelling openalex Transferable Save for project simulation_case UQ
Giuseppe Cataldo, Elizabeth Qian, Jeremy Auclair Journal of Astronomical Telescopes Instruments and Systems 2022-07-01 10.1117/1.jatis.8.3.038001
单位: NASA Goddard Space Flight Center, Greenbelt, Maryland, United States
通讯: Giuseppe Cataldo @ NASA Goddard Space Flight Center, Greenbelt, Maryland, United States
相关性: 该论文的多保真不确定性量化框架可迁移至生物力学植入物设计中的代理建模，但缺乏对约束处理和生物力学应用的直接验证。
边界: This paper does not address implant biomechanics, contact mechanics, or constraint handling in optimization; it focuses on aerospace systems with wavefront error as QoI.
动机· 大型多学科系统不确定性量化计算成本高，需高效方法。
方法· 提出多保真框架，结合低保真模型加速，高保真模型保证收敛。
结果· 全局灵敏度分析时间从2个月降至2天，且误差减小。
验证· 应用于詹姆斯·韦伯太空望远镜集成模型验证。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To enable tractable uncertainty quantification for large-scale multidisciplinary systems where high-fidelity simulations are computationally prohibitive.
Method· A multifidelity framework that leverages low-fidelity models for speed-up while retaining high-fidelity model in the loop for convergence, integrating mean/variance estimation and global sensitivity analysis.
Result· The framework reduced global sensitivity analysis time from over 2 months to under 2 days while improving accuracy of uncertainty estimates.
Validation· Applied to the James Webb Space Telescope integrated model for wavefront error prediction due to thermal distortions.
multifidelity · 多保真uncertainty quantification · 不确定性量化global sensitivity analysis · 全局灵敏度分析surrogate modeling · 代理建模high-fidelity model · 高保真模型low-fidelity model · 低保真模型wavefront error · 波前误差thermal distortion · 热变形
multifidelity uncertainty quantification global sensitivity analysis surrogate modeling aerospace
标记：

Proximal-exploration multi-objective Bayesian optimization for inverse identification of cyclic constitutive law of structural steels

Medium FEA & Surrogate Modelling openalex Transferable Save for project simulation_case
Bach Do, Makoto Ohsaki Structural and Multidisciplinary Optimization 2022-07-01 10.1007/s00158-022-03297-8
单位: Department of Architecture and Architectural Engineering, Graduate School of Engineering, Kyoto University, Kyoto-Daigaku Katsura, Nishikyo, Kyoto, 615-8540, Japan
通讯: Bach Do @ Department of Architecture and Architectural Engineering, Graduate School of Engineering, Kyoto University, Kyoto-Daigak
相关性: 该论文将多目标贝叶斯优化用于材料本构参数反演，方法可迁移至植入物力学设计中的参数识别问题。
边界: No FEA surrogate model, no implant or biomechanics context, and no uncertainty quantification beyond standard Bayesian optimization.
动机· 高效反演结构钢循环本构参数，避免昂贵实验试错。
方法· 提出近端探索多目标贝叶斯优化方法，平衡探索与利用。
结果· 在数值案例中成功识别参数，精度优于传统方法。
验证· 通过合成数据与有限元仿真验证反演效果。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To efficiently identify cyclic constitutive parameters of structural steels, reducing costly experimental trial-and-error.
Method· A proximal-exploration multi-objective Bayesian optimization algorithm is proposed to balance exploration and exploitation in parameter space.
Result· The method successfully identifies parameters in numerical cases with higher accuracy than conventional approaches.
Validation· Validation is performed using synthetic data and finite element simulations of cyclic loading.
multi-objective Bayesian optimization · 多目标贝叶斯优化inverse identification · 反演识别cyclic constitutive law · 循环本构定律structural steel · 结构钢proximal exploration · 近端探索finite element simulation · 有限元仿真parameter identification · 参数识别
multi-objective Bayesian optimization inverse identification cyclic constitutive law structural steel proximal exploration
标记：

An active learning Kriging-based method combining the weight information entropy function and the adaptive candidate sample pool

Medium FEA & Surrogate Modelling openalex Transferable Save for project numerical_benchmark UQ
Jingkui Li, Wenqi Liu, Yan Zhou, Zhandong Li Proceedings of the Institution of Mechanical Engineers Part O Journal of Risk and Reliability 2022-07-01 10.1177/1748006x221108825
单位: Civil Aviation College, Shenyang Aerospace University, Shenyang, China
通讯: Jingkui Li @ Civil Aviation College, Shenyang Aerospace University, Shenyang, China
相关性: 该方法通过主动学习Kriging模型提高可靠性分析效率，可迁移至生物力学植入物设计中的代理模型构建与不确定性量化。
边界: This paper focuses on reliability analysis with active learning Kriging, but does not address FEA surrogate modeling for implant biomechanics, constraint handling in multi-objective optimization, or patient-specific design.
动机· 降低Kriging模型可靠性分析中性能函数调用次数
方法· 结合权重信息熵函数与自适应候选样本池的主动学习方法
结果· 显著提高计算效率且不损失精度
验证· 四个数值算例和一个工程隐式函数算例验证
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To reduce the number of performance function calls in Kriging-based reliability analysis.
Method· An active learning method combining weight information entropy function and adaptive candidate sample pool via MCMC.
Result· The proposed method significantly improves computational efficiency without sacrificing accuracy.
Validation· Validated on four numerical examples and one engineering implicit performance function.
active learning · 主动学习Kriging model · Kriging模型reliability analysis · 可靠性分析information entropy · 信息熵Markov Chain Monte Carlo · 马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛limit state function · 极限状态函数design of experiments · 试验设计surrogate model · 代理模型
active learning Kriging reliability analysis information entropy adaptive candidate sample pool
标记：

Combining Constraint Solving and Bayesian Techniques for System Optimization

Medium FEA & Surrogate Modelling openalex Transferable Skim only simulation_case UQ
Franz Brauße, Zurab Khasidashvili, Konstantin Korovin Proceedings of the Thirty-First International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence 2022-07-01 10.24963/ijcai.2022/249
单位: The University of Manchester
通讯: Franz Brauße @ The University of Manchester
相关性: 该论文结合贝叶斯优化与约束求解，方法可迁移至植入物设计中的约束优化问题，但未涉及有限元代理模型或生物力学应用。
边界: This paper does not address surrogate modeling for expensive FEA, lacks uncertainty quantification beyond Bayesian optimization's inherent nature, and has no biomedical or implant design context.
动机· 工业系统优化中标准方法无法提供验证解和正确性保证。
方法· 结合贝叶斯优化与SMT约束求解，保证安全稳定解和最优性。
结果· 方法在工业系统上获得有最优性保证的稳定解。
验证· 通过工业系统案例验证方法有效性。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· Standard optimization techniques fail to provide validated solutions and correctness guarantees for complex industrial systems with explicit representations.
Method· A combination of Bayesian optimization and SMT-based constraint solving is proposed to achieve safe and stable solutions with optimality guarantees.
Result· The method yields stable solutions with optimality guarantees on industrial system optimization problems.
Validation· Validation is performed on industrial system case studies demonstrating the effectiveness of the combined approach.
Bayesian optimization · 贝叶斯优化constraint solving · 约束求解SMT · 可满足性模理论system optimization · 系统优化optimality guarantees · 最优性保证
Bayesian optimization constraint solving SMT system optimization industrial
标记：

Categorical-Continuous Bayesian optimization applied to chemical reactions (short version)

Medium FEA & Surrogate Modelling openalex Transferable Save for project numerical_benchmark UQ
Théo Rabut, Hamamache Kheddouci, Thomas Galeandro‐Diamant HAL (Le Centre pour la Communication Scientifique Directe) 2022-07-01 link
单位: UCBL - Université Claude Bernard Lyon 1 (43, boulevard du 11 novembre 1918, 69622 Villeurbanne cedex - France)
通讯: Théo Rabut @ UCBL - Université Claude Bernard Lyon 1 (43, boulevard du 11 novembre 1918, 69622 Villeurbanne cedex - France)
相关性: 该论文提出的分类-连续贝叶斯优化方法可迁移至植入物设计中的混合变量优化问题。
边界: No FEA validation, no biomedical context, no implant mechanics or biomechanics design application.
动机· 解决化学优化中混合变量（分类+连续）的采样效率问题。
方法· 提出一种处理分类-连续混合变量的贝叶斯优化框架。
结果· 在化学反应基准测试中验证了方法的有效性。
验证· 数值基准测试。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To address the sampling efficiency challenge in optimizing chemical reactions with mixed categorical and continuous variables.
Method· Proposes a Bayesian optimization framework that handles categorical-continuous mixed variables via a tailored kernel and acquisition function.
Result· Demonstrates improved optimization performance on benchmark chemical reaction problems.
Validation· Validated on numerical benchmark problems from chemical reaction optimization.
Bayesian optimization · 贝叶斯优化categorical variables · 分类变量mixed-variable optimization · 混合变量优化surrogate modeling · 代理建模chemical reactions · 化学反应
Bayesian optimization categorical variables chemical reactions surrogate modeling

Pushing the rheological and mechanical boundaries of extrusion-based 3D bioprinting.

Low AI for Bioprinting pubmed Peripheral Background only none Review
Liliang Ouyang Trends in biotechnology 2022-07-01 10.1016/j.tibtech.2022.01.001
单位: Department of Mechanical Engineering, Tsinghua University, Beijing 100084, China; Biomanufacturing and Rapid Forming Technology Key Laboratory of Beijing, Tsinghua University, Beijing 100084, China; "
通讯: Liliang Ouyang @ Department of Mechanical Engineering, Tsinghua University, Beijing 100084, China; Biomanufacturing and Rapid Forming Tec
相关性: 该综述讨论了生物打印中的流变学和力学边界，但未涉及任何AI方法。
边界: No AI integration into a bioprinting workflow stage; no experimental validation of any AI model.
动机· 克服生物打印中物理化学与生物学性能的权衡
方法· 综述近期在生物墨水流变学和打印物力学边界方面的进展

Motivation· To address the trade-offs between physicochemical and biological outcomes in 3D bioprinting by pushing rheological and mechanical boundaries.
Method· This review categorizes recent advances in bioink rheology and bioprint mechanics, focusing on unusual material properties and consolidation of strategies.
Result· It identifies expanded windows for material properties and provides perspectives on future boundaries in bioprinting.
Validation· No experimental validation; it is a review of existing literature.
bioink · 生物墨水rheology · 流变学extrusion-based bioprinting · 挤出式生物打印mechanical properties · 力学性能trade-off · 权衡bioprinting window · 生物打印窗口

标记：

The significance of biomacromolecule alginate for the 3D printing of hydrogels for biomedical applications.

Low AI for Bioprinting pubmed Peripheral Background only none Review
Kokkarachedu Varaprasad, Chandrasekaran Karthikeyan, Murali M Yallapu, Rotimi Sadiku International journal of biological macromolecules 2022-07-01 10.1016/j.ijbiomac.2022.05.157
单位: Facultad de Ingeniería y Tecnología, Universidad San Sebastián, Sede Concepción, Concepción, Bio-Bio, Chile.. Electronic address: prasad.kokkarachedu@uss.cl.
通讯: Kokkarachedu Varaprasad @ Facultad de Ingeniería y Tecnología, Universidad San Sebastián, Sede Concepción, Concepción, Bio-Bio, Chile.. Electronic  |  Murali M Yallapu @ Immunology & Microbiology Department, School of Medicine, University of Texas Rio Grande Valley (UTRGV), McAllen, TX, US
相关性: 该论文综述了海藻酸盐基水凝胶在3D生物打印中的应用，但未涉及AI方法或实验验证，与AI辅助生物打印方向关联较弱。
边界: This paper is a material-focused review with no AI integration, experimental validation, or autonomous decision-making components relevant to AI-driven bioprinting.
动机· 综述海藻酸盐基水凝胶在3D生物打印中的应用与改性。
方法· 系统回顾海藻酸盐的化学修饰及与其他材料的功能化复合。
结果· 总结了海藻酸盐生物墨水的发展现状与未来展望。
验证· 文献综述，无实际实验验证。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· This review aims to summarize the recent developments and significance of alginate-based biomaterial/bioink hydrogels for 3D extrusion bioprinting in biomedical applications.
Method· The authors comprehensively review the modification and functionalization of alginate with polymers and nanomaterials to improve printability and bioactivity.

单位: Hilase Centre, Institute of Physics of the Czech Academy of Sciences, Dolni Brezany, Czech Republic
通讯: Sunil Pathak @ Hilase Centre, Institute of Physics of the Czech Academy of Sciences, Dolni Brezany, Czech Republic
相关性: 该论文研究选择性激光熔化304L不锈钢的激光冲击强化后处理，属于金属增材制造，但未涉及生物医学植入物或骨整合相关性能。
边界: This paper does not address porous structures, stress shielding, fatigue testing, or osseointegration relevant to biomedical implants.
动机· 研究激光冲击强化对SLM成型SS-304L后处理效果的影响。
方法· 对四种不同状态的SLM试样进行激光冲击强化处理，测量残余应力和表面形貌。
结果· 激光冲击强化将残余拉应力转为压应力，最大达-1170 MPa，且深度达1 mm。

Motivation· To investigate the effect of laser shock peening as a post-processing technique on additively manufactured stainless steel SS-304L with different initial surface conditions.
Method· Four types of SLM-fabricated samples (as-built, annealed, polished, and combinations) were treated with laser shock peening, and the resulting compressive residual stresses and surface morphology were characterized.
Result· Laser shock peening significantly improved compressive residual stresses, converting tensile stresses to compressive with a maximum of -1170 MPa, remaining compressive up to 1 mm depth in most cases.
Validation· Validation was performed by measuring residual stress profiles via X-ray diffraction and surface morphology via microscopy.

单位: Key Laboratory of Advanced Materials (MOE), School of Material Science and Engineering, Tsinghua University, Beijing 100084, P.R. China
通讯: Amy Godfrey @ Key Laboratory of Advanced Materials (MOE), School of Material Science and Engineering, Tsinghua University, Beijing 100
相关性: 该论文研究316L不锈钢SLM样品的拉伸微观结构演变，未涉及多孔结构、应力遮挡或生物医学植入物相关性能。
边界: This paper does not address porous structure, stress shielding, fatigue testing, surface roughness, or osseointegration in the context of biomedical implants.
动机· 研究SLM制备316L不锈钢在拉伸过程中的微观结构演变和应变分布不均匀性。
方法· 结合宏观和微观数字图像相关（DIC）与电子背散射衍射（EBSD）进行多尺度分析。
结果· 发现塑性应变在多个尺度上非均匀分布，包括晶内滑移带形成，但未发现与晶粒尺寸或形态明显相关。

Motivation· To investigate the multi-scale strain distribution and microstructural evolution during tensile loading of 316L stainless steel fabricated by selective laser melting.
Method· A combined macro- and micro-digital image correlation (DIC) with electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) was employed to characterize strain heterogeneity and orientation evolution.
Result· Non-uniform plastic strain distribution was observed across multiple length scales, with localized slip bands at the intra-grain level, but no clear correlation with grain size or cellular structure orientation.
Validation· Experimental validation through in-situ tensile testing with simultaneous DIC and EBSD measurements.

单位: School of Mechanical Engineering, University of Campinas – UNICAMP, Campinas, 13083-860 SP, Brazil
通讯: Éder Sócrates Najar Lopes @ School of Mechanical Engineering, University of Campinas – UNICAMP, Campinas, 13083-860 SP, Brazil
相关性: 该论文研究工具钢的LPBF工艺，未涉及生物医学植入物、多孔结构或应力遮挡。
边界: No biomedical implant context, no porous structure, no stress shielding or stiffness matching, no fatigue testing, no osseointegration.
动机· 研究LPBF加工H13工具钢的瞬态温度场与微观组织演变。
方法· 建立有限元模型模拟激光扫描温度场，结合实验验证熔池尺寸。

英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To investigate the transient temperature field and microstructural evolution during LPBF of AISI H13 tool steel.
Method· A finite element model incorporating temperature-dependent properties, absorptivity profile, and anisotropic thermal conductivity was developed to simulate the melt pool and thermal history.
Result· The model predicted melt pool dimensions with less than 7% error and revealed untempered martensite in the final layer and tempered martensite in inner layers.
Validation· Experimental melt pool measurements and porosity analysis validated the numerical model.

方法· 采用高温SHPB测试获得等温真应力-应变曲线，拟合幂律和Johnson-Cook模型

Motivation· To provide accurate dynamic constitutive models of Ti-6Al-4V for finite element simulation of machining processes.
Method· High-temperature split Hopkinson pressure bar tests were conducted to obtain isothermal true stress–strain curves, and power-law and Johnson-Cook models were fitted.
Result· Both constitutive models achieved an average error of less than 6% compared to experimental results.
Validation· Experimental validation using SHPB data under various temperatures and strain rates.

Zhixin Liu, Weidong Liu, Weijie Ma AIP Advances 2022-07-01 10.1063/5.0098507
单位: China Automotive Technology and Research Center Co. Ltd. , Tianjin 300300, China
通讯: Weijie Ma @ China Automotive Technology and Research Center Co. Ltd. , Tianjin 300300, China
相关性: 该论文研究多孔结构的能量吸收和自恢复特性，但未涉及生物医学植入物的应力屏蔽或骨整合。
边界: No link to stress shielding or stiffness matching for biomedical implants; no fatigue testing; no surface roughness or osseointegration assessment.
动机· 研究不同多孔结构的能量吸收特性，解决传统加工难题。
方法· 通过LPBF制造五种多孔结构，结合实验和数值模拟分析。
结果· 五边形结构性能最优，比吸能达3331 mJ，所有结构在15%变形下形状恢复率超95%。

Result· The pentagonal structure exhibited the best specific energy absorption (3331 mJ) and crushing force efficiency (3165.74 J/kg), with all structures achieving over 95% shape recovery under 15% deformation.

单位: Jiangsu Provincial Engineering Laboratory for Laser Additive Manufacturing of High-Performance Metallic Components, College of Materials Science and Technology, Nanjing University of Aeronautics and A
通讯: Dongdong Gu @ Jiangsu Provincial Engineering Laboratory for Laser Additive Manufacturing of High-Performance Metallic Components, Coll
相关性: 该论文研究LPBF制备的陶瓷增强铝基复合材料，与生物医用植入物的应力屏蔽、疲劳或骨整合无关。

动机· 通过混合陶瓷增强相调控激光增材制造铝基复合材料的原位反应与强化效果。
方法· 采用LPBF制备(ZrC+TiC)/Al复合材料，研究激光功率对制造质量、显微硬度及磨损性能的影响。

Motivation· To regulate in-situ reactions and achieve effective strengthening in laser additive manufactured aluminum matrix composites by adding hybrid ceramic reinforcing phases.
Method· ZrC+TiC solid solution ceramic reinforced Al matrix composites were fabricated by LPBF, and the effects of laser power on manufacturing quality, microhardness, and wear properties were investigated.
Result· The 10 wt.% (ZrC+TiC)/Al composite achieved a tensile strength of ~227 MPa with uniform elongation of ~6.7%, three times higher than unreinforced Al.
Validation· Mechanical properties were validated through microhardness, tensile testing, and wear experiments.

Additive Manufacturing of Metallic Frameworks Supported by the All-on-Six Implant Concept: Dimensional Precision After Veneer Layering and Spark Erosion

Low AM Biomedical Materials openalex Peripheral Background only mechanical_testing Exp. validation
Daniele Valente Velôso, Thaís Barbin, Letícia Del Rio Silva, Guilherme Borges, Anna Gabriella Camacho Presotto et al. The International Journal of Oral & Maxillofacial Implants 2022-07-01 10.11607/jomi.9605
相关性: 该论文研究牙科植入物框架的制造精度，未涉及应力遮挡、疲劳或骨整合等核心方向。
边界: This paper focuses on dimensional precision of dental frameworks, not on porous structure, stress shielding, fatigue testing, or osseointegration relevant to AM biomedical implants.
动机· 比较SLM和EBM与铣削技术在All-on-Six种植体框架制造中的精度。
方法· 通过边缘密合度、螺丝松动扭矩和应变测量评估框架精度。
结果· SLM初始边缘密合度最差，但饰瓷后改善；EBM饰瓷后最差，但电火花后改善。
验证· 使用方差分析和Bonferroni检验进行统计比较。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To compare the dimensional precision of dental implant frameworks fabricated by selective laser melting and electron beam melting versus conventional milling.
Method· Dimensional precision was evaluated through marginal fit, screw-loosening torque, and strain gauge measurements at baseline, after veneer layering, and after spark erosion.
Result· SLM frameworks showed the worst marginal fit at baseline but improved after veneer layering, while EBM frameworks exhibited the worst fit after veneer layering but improved after spark erosion.
Validation· Statistical analysis was performed using two-way repeated-measures ANOVA with Bonferroni post-hoc test.

Hanna Nilsson Åhman, F. D’Elia, Pelle Mellin, Cecilia Persson Frontiers in Bioengineering and Biotechnology 2022-07-01 10.3389/fbioe.2022.917812
单位: Division of Biomedical Engineering, Department of Materials Science and Engineering, Uppsala University, Uppsala, Sweden
通讯: Hanna Nilsson Åhman @ Division of Biomedical Engineering, Department of Materials Science and Engineering, Uppsala University, Uppsala, Sweden
相关性: 该论文研究了镁合金的腐蚀行为，未涉及应力遮挡、疲劳或骨整合等生物力学性能。
边界: No porous structure design for stress shielding, no mechanical testing (fatigue or static), no surface roughness or osseointegration assessment.
动机· 理解PBF-LB加工镁合金的微观结构对腐蚀性能的影响
方法· 对比PBF-LB与挤压态Mg-Y-Nd-Zr合金的微观结构和腐蚀行为
结果· PBF-LB材料因二次相增多导致局部腐蚀速率更高，HIP后进一步加剧
验证· 通过电化学测试、质量变化和氢析出实验验证腐蚀行为
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To understand how the microstructure generated during PBF-LB affects the corrosion resistance of Mg-Y-Nd-Zr alloy for biodegradable implants.
Method· Microstructural characterization and corrosion testing (potentiodynamic polarization, mass change, hydrogen evolution) were performed on PBF-LB processed and extruded Mg-Y-Nd-Zr alloys, with and without hot isostatic pressing.
Result· The PBF-LB material exhibited higher localized corrosion rates due to increased secondary phases, which were further exacerbated by HIP; texture effects were masked by secondary phase contributions in long-term tests.
Validation· Corrosion behavior was validated through electrochemical and immersion tests comparing PBF-LB and extruded conditions.

Data‐Driven Discovery of Fokker‐Planck Equation for the Earth's Radiation Belts Electrons Using Physics‐Informed Neural Networks

Low FEA & Surrogate Modelling openalex Peripheral Background only none
Enrico Camporeale, George Wilkie, Alexander Drozdov, Jacob Bortnik Journal of Geophysical Research Space Physics 2022-07-01 10.1029/2022ja030377
单位: CIRES University of Colorado Boulder CO USA
通讯: Enrico Camporeale @ CIRES University of Colorado Boulder CO USA
相关性: 该论文使用PINN解决地球辐射带中的Fokker-Planck方程反问题，与生物力学或植入物设计中的FEA替代建模无直接关联。
边界: This paper applies PINN to space physics (radiation belt electron transport), not to biomedical FEA, surrogate modeling, or implant design; no constraints, UQ, or biomechanics context.
动机· 提高地球辐射带电子动力学模型的准确性
方法· 使用PINN求解Fokker-Planck方程反问题，推断扩散和漂移系数
结果· 发现漂移-扩散方程比纯扩散方程更准确，并给出系数参数化
验证· 使用范艾伦探测器4年数据验证
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To improve the accuracy of reduced models for radiation belt electron dynamics by inferring the governing equation from data.
Method· Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are employed to solve the inverse Fokker-Planck problem, simultaneously inferring diffusion and drift coefficients from satellite measurements.
Result· A drift-diffusion equation describes killer electron dynamics more accurately than the traditional diffusion equation, and a simpler L-only parameterization of coefficients is derived.
Validation· The method is validated using four years of Van Allen Probes data, showing improved fit compared to quasilinear diffusion models.

Margaret Lund, Daniel Champion 2022-07-01 10.2172/1874768
单位: Mission Support and Test Services, LLC (MSTS), North Las Vegas, NV (United States)
通讯: Margaret Lund @ Mission Support and Test Services, LLC (MSTS), North Las Vegas, NV (United States)
相关性: 该论文专注于数值模拟中的不确定性量化和灵敏度分析，但未涉及生物力学或植入物设计，与用户方向关联较弱。
边界: This paper does not address FEA surrogate modeling, implant biomechanics, or multi-objective constrained optimization; it focuses on forward sensitivity analysis for numerical error quantification in general simulation codes.
动机· 提高仿真中不确定性量化的效率，区分数值误差与物理参数不确定性。
方法· 扩展前向灵敏度分析方法，将时间步长和空间步长作为灵敏度参数。

英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To improve the efficiency of uncertainty quantification in simulations by separating numerical errors from physical parameter uncertainties.
Method· Extends forward sensitivity analysis by treating time step and spatial step as special sensitivity parameters to quantify discretization errors.
Result· The method enables systematic comparison of discretization errors against physical uncertainties and optimizes step sizes without compromising sensitivity results.
Validation· Verified on benchmark problems with manufactured solutions that have analytical forms for all sensitivities.

Wen Yang, Jun Huang, Zhaofei Tian, Guangliang Chen Journal of Physics Conference Series 2022-07-01 10.1088/1742-6596/2313/1/012006
单位: Institute for Standardization of Nuclear Industry, China Institute of Nuclear Industry Strategy, 43 Fucheng Road, 100048, Beijing, China
通讯: Wen Yang @ Institute for Standardization of Nuclear Industry, China Institute of Nuclear Industry Strategy, 43 Fucheng Road, 100048
相关性: 该论文使用确定性采样进行不确定性量化，但应用于核反应堆热工水力-中子学耦合模拟，与生物力学或植入物设计无关。
边界: No biomedical context, no implant mechanics, no FEA surrogate for solid mechanics, and no constraint handling or multi-objective optimization.
动机· 减少耦合模拟中不确定性量化所需的大量随机采样计算成本。
方法· 引入确定性采样方法进行不确定性量化与灵敏度分析，并在细网格级别实现。
结果· 确定性采样在保持精度的同时显著提高了计算效率，且输出不确定性在出口截面不同区域存在差异。
验证· 通过5×5棒束压水堆耦合模拟案例验证。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To reduce the high computational cost of random sampling in uncertainty quantification for coupled thermohydraulics-neutronics simulations.
Method· Deterministic sampling is introduced for uncertainty quantification and sensitivity analysis, implemented at fine-mesh level via a Python program.
Result· Deterministic sampling proves efficient while maintaining accuracy, and the uncertainty of outputs varies spatially across the outlet section.
Validation· Validated on a coupled CFD-MOC simulation of a 5×5 rod bundle in a pressurized water reactor.

动机· 减少语义分割所需的大量像素级标注。
方法· 结合主动学习和半监督学习，用熵选择少量专家标注像素并自动生成伪标签。
结果· 在生物医学和生物膜数据集上，仅用不到5%像素标注达到最优性能。

Result· The method achieves state-of-the-art segmentation performance on biomedical and biofilm datasets using less than 5% of pixel annotations.
Validation· Extensive experiments on biomedical and biofilm datasets with comparison to baselines.

Siva Gopal R International Journal of Statistics and Applied Mathematics 2022-07-01 10.22271/maths.2022.v7.i4c.2250
相关性: 该论文将随机微分方程与机器学习混合用于气象预测，与用户关注的生物力学代理模型和植入物设计无直接关联。
边界: This paper focuses on climate prediction with no FEA, no implant mechanics, no biomedical context, and no surrogate modeling for expensive simulations.
动机· 印度季风预测困难，传统数值模型和纯机器学习各有局限。
方法· 提出随机微分方程与LSTM混合框架，参数化漂移和扩散系数。
结果· 混合模型RMSE为2.1 mm，比纯随机模型提高34%，R²达0.967。
验证· 使用40年气象站数据验证，并与传统和纯机器学习模型对比。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· Accurate Indian monsoon prediction is challenging due to complex nonlinear dynamics and the need for uncertainty quantification.
Method· A hybrid framework integrating stochastic differential equations with LSTM neural networks is developed to parameterize drift and diffusion coefficients.
Result· The hybrid model achieves a daily rainfall RMSE of 2.1 mm, a 34% improvement over pure stochastic models, with an R² of 0.967.
Validation· Validation is performed on 40 years of station data (1980-2019) against traditional numerical, pure stochastic, and pure machine learning models.

单位: Biomedical Engineering and Biomechanics Research Centre, School of Engineering, National University of Ireland Galway, Ireland.
通讯: Ted J Vaughan @ Biomedical Engineering and Biomechanics Research Centre, School of Engineering, National University of Ireland Galway, I [推断]

动机· 预测全髋置换术后骨盆倾斜变化，以优化假体定位。

Motivation· To predict postoperative changes in pelvic tilt after total hip arthroplasty for improved implant positioning.

Modular dual-mobility articulations in patients with adverse spinopelvic mobility

Low Hip Implant & Femoral Stem openalex Peripheral Background only clinical Exp. validation
Rohit Dhawan, Jonathan Baré, Andrew Shimmin The Bone & Joint Journal 2022-07-01 10.1302/0301-620x.104b7.bjj-2021-1628.r1
单位: Melbourne Orthopaedic Group, Melbourne, Australia
通讯: Rohit Dhawan @ Melbourne Orthopaedic Group, Melbourne, Australia
相关性: 该论文关注髋关节置换术后脱位风险，未涉及股骨柄应力遮挡或微动分析。
边界: No biomechanical modeling, no stress shielding or micromotion quantification, no FEA or contact mechanics.
动机· 评估不良脊柱-骨盆活动度患者使用双动髋臼假体的早期生存率。
方法· 多中心前瞻性研究，纳入227例患者，分析澳大利亚关节置换登记处数据。
结果· 14个月时无脱位翻修，生存率99.1%。
验证· 基于临床登记处的随访数据验证。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To evaluate early survivorship of dual-mobility articulations in patients with adverse spinopelvic mobility undergoing primary THA.
Method· Multicenter prospective study of 227 patients with adverse spinopelvic parameters, using AOANJRR data for follow-up.
Result· No dislocation revisions were reported at mean 14 months, with a Kaplan-Meier survival rate of 99.1%.
Validation· Clinical registry-based follow-up with revision as endpoint.

β duplex phase Ti–Zr–Nb–Ag alloys with impressive mechanical properties, excellent antibacterial and good biocompatibility

Low Hip Implant & Femoral Stem openalex Peripheral Background only mechanical_testing Exp. validation
Xiaotong Bao, Talante Juma, Xuan Li, Jili Ding, Xiaobin Liu et al. Journal of Materials Research and Technology 2022-07-01 10.1016/j.jmrt.2022.07.007
单位: Key Laboratory of Aerospace Materials and Performance (Ministry of Education), School of Materials Science and Engineering, Beihang University, Beijing 100191, China
通讯: Yongping Cao @ Orthopedic Department, Peking University First Hospital, Beijing 100034, China  |  Tao Zhang @ Center for Advanced Analysis and Computational Science, Zhengzhou University, Zhengzhou 450001, China
相关性: 该论文开发了低杨氏模量的β双相钛合金，旨在减少应力遮挡效应，但未涉及股骨柄植入物的力学建模或验证。
边界: No hip implant design, no FEA modeling, no micromotion or stress shielding quantification in Gruen zones, no patient-specific or intact vs implanted comparison.
动机· 开发低模量钛合金以减少应力遮挡效应
方法· 快速凝固法制备β双相Ti-Zr-Nb-Ag合金并表征性能
结果· 合金杨氏模量60-61 GPa，Ag5合金抗菌率99.1%
验证· 力学测试、离子释放、细胞实验验证
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To develop a low Young's modulus titanium alloy for reducing stress shielding in orthopedic implants.
Method· A series of β duplex phase Ti50Zr25Nb25-xAgx alloys were fabricated by rapid solidification and characterized for microstructure, mechanical, antibacterial, and biocompatibility properties.
Result· The alloys exhibited a Young's modulus of 60-61 GPa, high strength, and the Ag5 alloy achieved 99.1% antibacterial rate.
Validation· Mechanical testing, ion release analysis, and cell live/dead staining experiments were performed.
β duplex phase · β双相Young's modulus · 杨氏模量stress shielding · 应力遮挡rapid solidification · 快速凝固antibacterial · 抗菌biocompatibility · 生物相容性

标记：

Modified acetabular component liner designs are not superior to standard liners at reducing the risk of revision

Low Hip Implant & Femoral Stem openalex Peripheral Background only clinical
Paula Krull, Arnd Steinbrück, Alexander Grimberg, Oliver Melsheimer, Michael M. Morlock et al. The Bone & Joint Journal 2022-07-01 10.1302/0301-620x.104b7.bjj-2021-1791.r1
单位: Charite – Center for Musculosceletal Surgery, Berlin, Germany
通讯: Paula Krull @ Charite – Center for Musculosceletal Surgery, Berlin, Germany
相关性: 该论文关注髋臼内衬设计对翻修率的影响，未涉及股骨柄应力遮挡或微动分析。
边界: This paper does not quantify proximal femur stress/strain, micromotion, or contact mechanics; it is a registry-based clinical study on acetabular liner revision rates.
动机· 评估改良髋臼内衬设计是否降低机械并发症翻修率
方法· 分析德国关节置换登记处151,096例初次全髋置换数据，采用竞争风险分析和Cox回归
结果· 偏移型内衬降低翻修风险，成角偏移型增加风险，标准型仍为良好选择
验证· 基于大规模临床登记数据的统计分析
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To investigate whether modified acetabular polyethylene liner designs reduce the risk of revision for mechanical complications in primary cementless total hip arthroplasty.
Method· A registry-based analysis of 151,096 primary cementless THAs from the German Arthroplasty Registry using competing risk analysis and Cox proportional-hazards modeling.
Result· Offset liners reduced revision risk (HR 0.68), while angulated/offset liners increased risk (HR 1.81); standard liners remain a good choice.
Validation· Clinical validation through large-scale registry data with competing risk and Cox regression.
acetabular liner · 髋臼内衬total hip arthroplasty · 全髋关节置换术revision risk · 翻修风险registry study · 登记研究competing risk analysis · 竞争风险分析Cox proportional-hazards model · Cox比例风险模型
hip implant acetabular liner revision risk registry study

Niels G A Willemen, Shabir Hassan, Melvin Gurian, Maria Fernanda Jasso-Salazar, Kai Fan et al. Advanced healthcare materials 2022-07-01 10.1002/adhm.202102697

通讯: Su Ryon Shin @ Division of Engineering in Medicine, Department of Medicine, Harvard Medical School, and Brigham and Women's Hospital, C [推断]
相关性: 该论文不涉及任何人工智能方法，与AI生物打印方向无关。
边界: This paper focuses on biochemical methods to reduce peroxide toxicity in oxygen-generating biomaterials and does not incorporate AI into any bioprinting workflow stage.
动机· 解决自供氧生物材料中过氧化氢毒性问题
方法· 比较三种过氧化氢酶加入方式，选择最佳方案制成生物墨水
结果· 游离过氧化氢酶显著降低过氧化氢水平，提高细胞活性
验证· 通过细胞活性和代谢活性验证效果
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To alleviate the toxic effects of hydrogen peroxide in oxygen-releasing biomaterials used for implant survival.
Method· Three distinct catalase incorporation methods were compared to determine the most effective formulation for scavenging H2O2 in oxygen-generating hydrogels.
Result· Free catalase in the hydrogel significantly reduced H2O2 levels and improved cellular viability and metabolic activity.
Validation· In vitro cell viability and metabolic activity assays were performed using printed constructs under hypoxic conditions.

通讯: Wenxian Gu @ Department of Pathology, The Affiliated Changzhou No. 2 People's Hospital of Nanjing Medical University, Changzhou 21300  |  Zhongzhi Jia @ Department of Interventional and Vascular Surgery, The Affiliated Changzhou No. 2 People's Hospital of Nanjing Medical U

动机· 研究芍药苷-海藻酸钠-明胶皮肤支架对糖尿病创面的疗效。

CyMAD bioreactor: A cyclic magnetic actuation device for magnetically mediated mechanical stimulation of 3D bioprinted hydrogel scaffolds.

Exclude AI for Bioprinting pubmed Peripheral Ignore mechanical_testing Exp. validation
Charis Czichy, David Kilian, Tzu-Chia Wang, Stefan Günther, Anja Lode et al. Journal of the mechanical behavior of biomedical materials 2022-07-01 10.1016/j.jmbbm.2022.105253
单位: Chair of Magnetofluiddynamics, Measuring and Automation Technology, Technische Universität Dresden, George-Bähr-Strasse 3, 01069, Dresden, Germany.
通讯: Stefan Odenbach @ Chair of Magnetofluiddynamics, Measuring and Automation Technology, Technische Universität Dresden, George-Bähr-Strasse
相关性: 该论文描述了一种用于生物打印支架的磁力刺激生物反应器，但没有涉及任何人工智能技术，与用户的研究方向不相关。
边界: This paper does not incorporate any AI or machine learning into the bioprinting workflow; it focuses solely on the design and validation of a magnetic actuation bioreactor for mechanical stimulation.
动机· 开发一种无接触的磁力刺激装置，以增强生物打印支架中细胞的分化。
方法· 设计并制造基于Maxwell构型的循环磁刺激生物反应器，对含磁性微粒的水凝胶进行力学刺激。
结果· 经过14天刺激，支架实现了约9%的伸长，且细胞相容性良好。
验证· 通过μCT和数字图像处理验证了粒子分布均匀性和变形行为。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To develop a contactless mechanical stimulation device for enhancing cell differentiation in bioprinted hydrogel scaffolds.
Method· A cyclic magnetic actuation bioreactor based on Maxwell coil configuration was designed and fabricated to stimulate magnetically responsive hydrogels.
Result· Cyclic stimulation over 14 days achieved approximately 9% elongation of the scaffolds with maintained cytocompatibility.
Validation· Particle distribution and deformation were validated using μCT and digital image processing.

Exclude AM Biomedical Materials openalex Peripheral Ignore mechanical_testing Exp. validation
Xinheng Li, Yiwen Wu, Rui Yang, Zihui Zhang IOP Conference Series Materials Science and Engineering 2022-07-01 10.1088/1757-899x/1249/1/012016
单位: Center for Adaptive System Engineering, ShanghaiTech University, Shanghai 201210, China
通讯: Zihui Zhang @ Center for Adaptive System Engineering, ShanghaiTech University, Shanghai 201210, China
相关性: 该论文研究的是Inconel 718高温合金的应变局部化机制，与生物医学植入物、应力遮挡、多孔结构或骨整合无关。
边界: This paper does not address porous structures, stress shielding, stiffness matching, LPBF fabrication of biomedical implants, mechanical testing (fatigue or static), surface roughness, or osseointegration.
动机· 探究激光粉末床熔融Inconel 718合金中孪晶界应变局部化的起源。
方法· 采用原位高分辨率数字图像相关（HRDIC）和关联表征技术分析应变分布。
结果· 发现V形γ″析出相沿孪晶界形成无析出区，导致局部塑性应变集中。
验证· 通过HRDIC应变图和微观结构表征验证。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To investigate the mechanistic origin of strain localization at twin boundaries in Inconel 718 superalloy fabricated by laser powder bed fusion.
Method· In-situ high-resolution digital image correlation (HRDIC) combined with correlative characterization of precipitates and dislocation slip bands.
Result· V-shaped γ″ precipitates along twin boundaries create precipitate-denuded zones, leading to localized plasticity and severe strain localization.
Validation· Experimental validation via HRDIC strain mapping and microstructural analysis.

英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To determine which temporal instance queries can be uniquely characterized by a polynomial-size set of positive and negative examples.
Method· Analyze the characterizability of queries in fragments of LTL and 2D languages combining LTL with description logics.
Result· Path-shaped queries admit polynomial characterizations, and the scope of temporal operators affects characterizability.
Validation· Theoretical proofs without experimental validation.

Changyi Liu, Lingshuai Huang, Hanyang Zhang, Fei Chang, Shichao Li et al. Journal of the mechanical behavior of biomedical materials 2022-07-01 10.1016/j.jmbbm.2022.105260
单位: Key Laboratory of Bionic Engineering, Ministry of Education, Jilin University, Changchun, 130025, PR China; Weihai Institute for Bionics-Jilin University, Weihai, 264207, PR China.
通讯: Hanyang Zhang @ Orthopedics Department, Second Hospital of Jilin University, Changchun, 130025, PR China. Electronic address: doctorzhan
相关性: 与用户研究方向无关，论文仅涉及足部跖骨截骨螺钉的生物力学比较，不涉及髋关节植入物或股骨柄。
边界: This paper evaluates foot osteotomy screws, not hip implants; no stress shielding analysis in Gruen zones, no micromotion modeling, no femoral stem mechanics.
动机· 比较可吸收PLA螺钉与钛螺钉在拇外翻截骨术中的力学性能。
方法· 使用3D打印截骨导板和人工模型进行静力和疲劳实验，采用数字图像相关测量全场应变。
结果· PLA螺钉在合适截骨角下压缩强度与钛螺钉相当，刚度达60%-90%，循环后仍保持强度。
验证· 机械测试与DIC验证。
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To compare the mechanical properties of bioabsorbable PLA screws and titanium screws in hallux valgus chevron osteotomy.
Method· Artificial first metatarsal models were cut using 3D-printed guides and fixed with either PLA or titanium screws, then subjected to static and fatigue compression while full-field strain was measured with digital image correlation.
Result· PLA screws achieved compressive strength comparable to titanium screws under appropriate osteotomy angles, with stiffness reaching 60-90% of titanium screws, and maintained integrity after 240,000 cycles.
Validation· Mechanical testing using digital image correlation on synthetic bone models.

Motivation· To investigate how surgical approach and bearing size/type influence dislocation risk in total hip arthroplasty for femoral neck fractures.
Method· An observational cohort study of 8,031 patients using Swedish Arthroplasty Register and National Patient Register data with Kaplan-Meier and Cox regression.

Validation· Clinical registry data analysis with multivariable regression adjustment.

单位: Department of Teaching, Research and Development, Schulthess Klinik, Zurich, Switzerland
通讯: Vincent A. Stadelmann @ Department of Teaching, Research and Development, Schulthess Klinik, Zurich, Switzerland

结果· 关节囊保留组患者报告结果略差，但差异无临床意义

Motivation· To determine whether preservation and anatomical closure of the anterolateral capsule improves patient-reported outcomes and revision rates in direct anterior approach total hip arthroplasty compared to capsule resection.
Method· Two consecutive patient cohorts (capsule resected n=430, capsule preserved n=450) were compared using generalized multiple regression analysis of Oxford Hip Score and COMI-Hip at two years postoperatively.

Motivation· To describe the anterior-based muscle-sparing approach for total hip arthroplasty and its proposed advantages in reducing dislocation risk.
Method· The procedure is performed supine via a minimally invasive Watson-Jones approach with optional intraoperative fluoroscopy for component positioning.
Result· The anterolateral approach shows lower dislocation rates compared to the posterior approach, and supine positioning facilitates leg length and cup position assessment.
Validation· Validation relies on cited literature dislocation rates; no original biomechanical or clinical validation is presented.

单位: Department of Orthopaedics, Government Medical College, University of Kashmir, Srinagar, Jammu & Kashmir, India
通讯: Seeyan Mustafa Shah @ Department of Orthopaedics, Government Medical College, University of Kashmir, Srinagar, Jammu & Kashmir, India
相关性: 该论文仅关注术前规划模板的准确性，未涉及股骨柄应力遮挡、微动或接触力学等生物力学分析。
边界: This paper does not quantify stress shielding, micromotion, or bone-implant interface mechanics; it only evaluates radiographic templating accuracy for component sizing.
动机· 评估基于X线片的术前规划模板预测假体尺寸和恢复偏移的准确性。
方法· 对18例全髋关节置换术患者进行术前模板测量，并与术中实际使用假体及术后X线片比较。

Motivation· To evaluate the accuracy of an analog preoperative templating method using conventional radiographs for predicting component sizes and restoring offset in primary total hip arthroplasty.
Method· Preoperative templating was performed on 18 patients by overlaying prosthesis transparencies on radiographs; predicted sizes and displacements were compared with intraoperative components and postoperative radiographs.
Result· The templating method achieved 68.5% accuracy in predicting both acetabular and femoral component sizes.
Validation· Validation was performed by comparing templated predictions with actual implanted component sizes and postoperative radiographic measurements.

Motivation· To evaluate psychiatric disorders and genotoxicity in metal-on-polyethylene total hip arthroplasty patients and their correlation with cobalt/chromium levels.

Method· Treatment involved component retention, bone grafting, and cementation of a dual-mobility liner into the acetabular component.

Validation· Clinical follow-up without experimental or numerical validation.

To Study the Impact of Different Optimization Methods on Intensity-Modulated Radiotherapy and Volumetric-Modulated Arc Therapy Plans for Hip Prosthesis Patients

Exclude Hip Implant & Femoral Stem openalex Peripheral Ignore clinical Patient-specific
Pawan Kumar Singh, Deepak Tripathi, Sukhvir Singh, Manindra Bhushan, Lalit Kumar et al. Journal of Medical Physics 2022-07-01 10.4103/jmp.jmp_14_22
单位: Amity Institute of Applied Sciences, Amity University, Noida, Uttar Pradesh, India
通讯: Pawan Kumar Singh @ Amity Institute of Applied Sciences, Amity University, Noida, Uttar Pradesh, India
相关性: 该论文研究放疗计划优化方法，未涉及髋关节假体的生物力学或应力遮挡分析。
边界: This paper focuses on radiotherapy planning optimization for hip prosthesis patients, not on biomechanical analysis, stress shielding, micromotion, or implant stability.
动机· 研究不同优化方法对髋假体患者放疗计划的影响
方法· 对16例金属髋假体患者生成三组IMRT和VMAT计划，比较剂量分布
结果· 限制射束进入假体的优化方法改善了靶区覆盖和均匀性
验证· 临床患者数据比较
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To investigate the impact of different optimization strategies on radiotherapy plan quality for patients with metallic hip implants.
Method· Three sets of IMRT and VMAT plans were generated for 16 patients, with restrictions on beam entry and exit relative to the implant.
Result· Restricting beam entry improved target coverage and homogeneity for both IMRT and VMAT, with VMAT plans showing superior volumetric coverage.
Validation· Clinical patient cohort comparison.

The Obturator Externus as Surgical Landmark for the Direct Anterior Approach and Its Role in LLD after Total Hip Replacement

Exclude Hip Implant & Femoral Stem openalex Peripheral Ignore clinical
Lieven Vermuyten, Ronald B. Driesen, Hans Welters, Kristoff Corten Journal of Arthroscopy and Joint Surgery 2022-07-01 10.4103/jajs.jajs_70_22
单位: Department of Orthopedic Surgery, ZOL, Genk, Belgium
通讯: Lieven Vermuyten @ Department of Orthopedic Surgery, ZOL, Genk, Belgium
相关性: 该论文仅关注全髋置换术后下肢长度差异的临床预测，未涉及股骨柄应力遮挡或微动等生物力学分析。
边界: This paper does not quantify proximal femur stress/strain, micromotion, or contact mechanics; it is purely clinical with no FEA or biomechanical modeling.
动机· 评估闭孔外肌腱作为标志物对全髋置换术后下肢长度差异的预测价值
方法· 回顾性分析85例患者术后X线片，测量股骨柄与闭孔外肌腱的距离并进行回归分析
结果· 闭孔外肌腱距离大于6mm时，术后下肢延长超过5mm的相对风险为11.2
验证· 通过组内相关系数评估观察者间和观察者内可靠性
英文摘要与术语
Motivation· To assess the utility of the obturator externus tendon as a surgical landmark for predicting leg length discrepancy after direct anterior approach total hip replacement.
Method· A retrospective radiographic analysis of 85 patients was performed, measuring the distance from the femoral component to the obturator externus insertion and correlating it with postoperative leg length difference using regression and discriminant analysis.
Result· An obturator externus distance greater than 6 mm was associated with a relative risk of 11.2 for leg lengthening exceeding 5 mm.
Validation· Intra- and interobserver reliability was assessed using the intraclass correlation coefficient, which showed excellent agreement.
obturator externus tendon · 闭孔外肌腱leg length discrepancy · 下肢长度差异total hip replacement · 全髋关节置换术direct anterior approach · 直接前入路femoral stem position · 股骨柄位置
total hip replacement leg length discrepancy obturator externus direct anterior approach
